Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on spin structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 967. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 995 with relations in degree 3.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by spin generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 967, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 995. QED.
